disposal GOVERNS.** **A `begin` whose session is disposed during the call must NOT report success: it refuses `{ok:false, code:'disposed'}` — the EXISTING SEVEN-MEMBER closed-domain code, with NO EIGHTH MEMBER added — and the refused attempt consumes NOTHING: the discarded record stays DISCARDED (`gesture()` stays `null`), the gesture COUNTER is NOT consumed (`stats().gestures` stays at its pre-call value, `0` on a fresh session), the handle ID is NOT consumed (`stats().gestureId` stays `0`), `stats().active` stays `false`, the ledger stays EMPTIED (`stats().installed === 0`, item 7.3) and the baseline stays restored (item 7.2, `I-5`: no listener of any type survives a begin that reported success would be the only way listeners could come back).** **THE PRECEDENCE, STATED UNAMBIGUOUSLY SO A READER DERIVING A ROW CANNOT HESITATE: the disposal is checked and RE-CHECKED against the session's own live state at the END of the establishment window and OWNS the outcome of that call — `'disposed'` OUTRANKS `'ok'` (and outranks `'busy'`, `'not-installed'` and `'disconnected'`, as item 7.3's and `P-GS-IM-3`'s precedence clauses already state) — so a begin that completes its establishment steps and then finds the session disposed during its own window reports the REFUSAL, never a success; the same rule binds the case where the disposal lands from `onStart` (item 1(d): a throwing `onStart` propagates, while a DISPOSING one is the refusal above).** **The module's matching fix follows the parallel red row (`ADV-GS-30`'s regression row, authored red-first by the TestWriter) and is the Implementer's; this clause is the CONTRACT it is written against.** **SILENCE, recorded rather than silently resolved: this clause pins NO `lastCode` cell for a dispose-governed refusal** — `§2.1`'s `lastCode` cell reads *"the LAST result code this session produced"*, so a row that asserts `lastCode === 'disposed'` after this refusal is DERIVING it from that cell rather than citing a clause written for this path. **NO id, strategy id, attempt term, the `396` total, the seed, the caps, the `12`-name export census, the seven-member union or any section number moves for this clause.**
4. **A throwing `off` is caught per call:** the remaining detaches still run, `DisposeReport.removed` counts the **successful** detaches, and **`complete: false`** is reported — an honest *"the baseline could not be fully restored"*, never a false restore (`§0A` note 3).
5. **`dispose()` is IDEMPOTENT:** the second call performs **zero** source calls and reports **`{removed: 0, complete: true}`**.
6. **`dispose()` never throws**, whatever the source does; and **the listener baseline it restores is its OWN** — the session claims **nothing** about listeners another party installed (layer anchor 5).

7.7 **⟶ ADDED 2026-09-27 (the `ADV-GS-30` amendment — the MID-CALL DISPOSAL, stated as a CLAUSE; a `§2.3`-side clause numbered `7.7`, NOT a `§5.5.1` register row and NOT a new code): THE DISPOSAL GOVERNS A `begin` WHOSE SESSION IS DISPOSED DURING THAT CALL.** **The measured behaviour this clause replaces, kept visible because it is the reading a later pass could otherwise re-derive from the pre-fix bytes: a `begin` whose session is disposed DURING the call (from `capturePointer`, from `onStart`, or from ANY consumer code running inside that window — item 1(d)'s establishment order puts both of them inside `begin`) returned `{ok:true}` on a session that is `disposed === true` (measured: `stats().active === false`, `gestureId === 0`, `gesture() === null`, `gestures === 1`, `lastCode === 'ok'`, and a handle reading `active:false` / `outcome:'cancel'`) — i.e. a begin that REPORTS SUCCESS on a session that cannot accept one.** **⟶ EXTENDED 2026-09-27 (THE GATE-7 PROOFREADER PASS — the DISCARD LIMB, added here so this clause is citable, because the discard-shape row in `tests/gesture-session.test.ts` cites `§2.3` item 7.7's discard limb and until this annotation the discard shape was recorded ONLY at `§3b.0` as `DIS-1`, where it could not be cited by a behaviour row): THIS ITEM NOW CARRIES TWO LIMBS, and both refuse with the EXISTING seven-member `'disposed'` code (no eighth member is invented, `§2.2`/`§4.4 S-9`) — (i) THE DISPOSAL SHAPE, the clause body above: the session is DISPOSED during the call (from `capturePointer`, from `onStart`, or from any consumer code running inside that window), so `ended` is true; and (ii) THE DISCARD SHAPE, `§3b.0`'s `DIS-1` stated in THIS clause's own terms: the attempt's OWN record is DISCARDED during the establishment order by consumer code running inside it — e.g. `cancel(element)` called from the consumer's own `onStart` hook — WITHOUT the session being disposed and WITHOUT `ended` becoming true. THE BODY OF THE DISCARD LIMB IS NOT WRITTEN A SECOND TIME HERE: it stands ONCE at `§3b.0` (`DIS-1`), which is this file's clause of record for it, and this cell is the `§2.3`-side pointer a TestWriter cites; the discipline is item 7.3's own tombstone/pointer rule (ONE site carries the body). NEITHER the numbering nor the `§2.3` clause list moves: item 7.7 keeps its number, NO `7.8` is created, items 7.1–7.7 keep their order, and no register row, attempt term, the `396` total, the seed, the caps, the strategy ids, the `12`-name census or the SEVEN-member union moves for this annotation.**) — i.e. a begin that REPORTS SUCCESS on a session that cannot accept one.** **THE RULING: the disposal GOVERNS.** **A `begin` whose session is disposed during the call must NOT report success: it refuses `{ok:false, code:'disposed'}` — the EXISTING SEVEN-MEMBER closed-domain code, with NO EIGHTH MEMBER added — and the refused attempt consumes NOTHING: the discarded record stays DISCARDED (`gesture()` stays `null`), the gesture COUNTER is NOT consumed (`stats().gestures` stays at its pre-call value, `0` on a fresh session), the handle ID is NOT consumed (`stats().gestureId` stays `0`), `stats().active` stays `false`, the ledger stays EMPTIED (`stats().installed === 0`, item 7.3) and the baseline stays restored (item 7.2, `I-5`: a begin that reported success would be the only way listeners could come back).** **THE PRECEDENCE, STATED UNAMBIGUOUSLY SO A READER DERIVING A ROW CANNOT HESITATE: the disposal is checked and RE-CHECKED against the session's own live state at the END of the establishment window and OWNS the outcome of that call — `'disposed'` OUTRANKS `'ok'` (and outranks `'busy'`, `'not-installed'` and `'disconnected'`, as item 7.3's and `P-GS-IM-3`'s precedence clauses already state) — so a begin that completes its establishment steps and then finds the session disposed during its own window reports the REFUSAL, never a success; the same rule binds the case where the disposal lands from `onStart` (item 1(d): a THROWING `onStart` propagates to the caller of `begin`, while a DISPOSING one is the refusal above).** **The module's matching fix follows the parallel red row (`ADV-GS-30`'s regression row, authored red-first by the TestWriter) and is the Implementer's; this clause is the CONTRACT it is written against.** **SILENCE, recorded rather than silently resolved: this clause pins NO `lastCode` cell for a dispose-governed refusal** — `§2.1`'s `lastCode` cell reads *"the LAST result code this session produced"*, so a row asserting `lastCode === 'disposed'` after this refusal is DERIVING it from that cell rather than citing a clause written for this path. **NO id, strategy id, attempt term, the `396` total, the seed, the caps, the `12`-name export census, the seven-member union or any section number moves for this clause.**

**Item 8 — THE STATE MACHINE, in four states (this is the whole state space).**

| State | Meaning | Reachable by |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **`absent`** | no gesture and no installed control | the instance's initial state; every terminal; a cancel; any refusal |
| **`installed-idle`** | ≥1 control installed, no gesture active | a successful `install`; every terminal; a cancel; a refusal while controls exist |
| **`active`** | exactly ONE gesture in flight | a successful `begin` |
| **`disposed`** | terminal, irreversible | `dispose()` |

**Legal transitions:** `absent → installed-idle → active → installed-idle` (**all four paths: `end`, `reset`, `cancel`, a refused terminal**), and **any** state `→ disposed`. **Illegal, and therefore refused with their codes:** `active → active` (`'busy'`); `disposed → installed-idle` (`'disposed'`); `absent → active` (`'not-installed'`); `active → installed-idle` via an `end`/`reset` carrying a **stale** handle (`'stale'`, and the gesture **stays active** — a refusal never half-terminates). **The instance's counters are monotonic across every transition** (`§0A` note 8). **⟶ CROSS-REFERENCE ADDED 2026-09-27 (the `ADV-GS-30` amendment — the precedence this table needs for the mid-call case): the `disposed` state is RE-CHECKED against the session's own live state at the END of a `begin`'s establishment window (item 7.7), so an attempt that STARTED in `absent`/`installed-idle` and was disposed DURING the call reports the `installed-idle → disposed` REFUSAL `'disposed'` and never the `absent/installed-idle → active` transition — the transition this table declares is the one the call COMPLETES with, and the disposal governs it.**

**⟶ RECORDED 2026-09-27 (the `D-6`/`ADV-GS-6` amendment — the ACTUAL cancellation contract, and the one-word-one-meaning observation).** **`cancel`'s element mismatch is NOT `end`/`reset`'s stale-handle refusal, and the two codes are both derivable from the clauses as they stand:** **`end`/`reset` on a handle that is not the active gesture's refuse `'stale'`** (the handle is identity-checked, `§2.4` item 4 / `§2.3` item 4's terminal row), **while `cancel(element, gesture?)` refuses `'no-gesture'` when the passed `element` is not the ACTIVE gesture's own element** (`cancel`'s precondition is "`gesture` omitted, **or** the active handle" — `§2.3` item 4 — and the element is part of that precondition, so a mismatching element is read as **"no gesture of mine is at the element you named"**), and **only then, if the element DOES match and a handle was supplied that is not the active one, `'stale'`.** **The closed union is unchanged — SEVEN members, no eighth (`§4.4 S-9`), NO new code, NO new method and NO behaviour change:** `P-GS-IM-3`'s state `(4)` (`the call passed a STALE handle`) therefore asserts **`'stale'` for `end`/`reset` and `'stale'` for `cancel` on a stale handle at the ACTIVE element, and `'no-gesture'` for a `cancel` whose ELEMENT does not match** — and both are refusals that are INERT (`commit` zero times, no hook, no source call, the active gesture untouched). **THE OBSERVATION, recorded rather than smoothed over: one word does not carry one meaning here — `'no-gesture'` means "there is no active gesture" in `F-5`'s idle case and "the element you named is not the active gesture's" in this one** (an element-mismatch reading is arguably a *stale*-class refusal, since the caller named a target that is not live). **⟶ ARCHITECT QUESTION, recorded as `§7a.1` item 6:** whether `cancel`'s element mismatch should be re-lexed to `'stale'` (which would need an explicit clause in this item, not a silent re-reading) or the two-meaning use of `'no-gesture'` accepted as the contract. **This amendment INVENTS NO BEHAVIOUR: it records what the landed module does, against the clauses above, and no row may be written for the other reading until an architect rules.**

### 2.4 The value, validity and event rules — how the session treats what it is handed

**Item 1 — the events are OPAQUE, and the session reads NO field of any event.** The session's four handlers take **no argument they use**; `pointerId`, `button`, `buttons`, `isPrimary`, `clientX`/`clientY` and every coordinate are **never read, never compared, never stored** (`§0A` note 4, `§0` ruling 7, `R-8`). **Consequence, stated because it is the magnitude clause in mechanical form: no pointer coordinate reaches the session, so the session cannot know a distance, a delta or a magnitude — and no pass may claim it does.**

**Item 2 — the element is compared BY IDENTITY.** `install`'s ledger is keyed by the element object (identity), never by an id, class, attribute, position or name. **Two structurally identical but distinct objects are two controls; the same object is the same control.** A repeated `install` on the same element is a **no-op returning `false`**, and **the config of the FIRST install stays in force** — *"a second `install` does not double listeners"* is satisfied **structurally** (no second `on` call can happen), which is stronger than a de-dup check.

**Item 3 — one gesture per session; a second `pointerdown` does not start one.** `'busy'` is returned and **nothing changes** (`§2.3` item 3). **A `pointermove` arriving between gestures has no handler attached at all** (the listeners do not exist outside the window), so it is a no-op **by construction, not by a guard** — this is the *"gesture-scoped tracking listeners opened ONLY between start and end"* clause made mechanical.

**Item 4 — the gesture id is monotonic and NEVER reused.** `id` starts at `1`, increments on every successful `begin`, **never resets** (not even after `dispose()`), and **a handle is valid only for the ONE active gesture with that id AND that element identity**. **A stale handle's terminal is refused `'stale'` and does NOT touch the active gesture** — so *"a stale handle's `end` terminates the live gesture"* is the `F-6` fail-state, and it is refusable-by-design rather than a judgement call.

**Item 5 — connectivity: one reading, one failing answer.** At `begin`, **iff** `source.isConnected` is callable, the session calls **`isConnected(element)` exactly once**. **Returning exactly `false` ⇒ `{ok:false, code:'disconnected'}`**, and **no listener is attached, no gesture is created, no commit can occur.** **Any other outcome — `true`, `undefined`, `null`, `0`, `''`, a non-boolean, a throw, or an ABSENT `isConnected` — means "no contrary evidence"** and the gesture proceeds (`§0A` note 9). **This is the one connectivity gate the A-d3 shape allows**, and it is deliberately **narrow**: an absent seam must not make the session inert, and a throwing seam must not crash a gesture.

**Item 6 — an UNUSABLE source is the valid-state degradation, never a throw.** "Unusable" is exact: the source is **not an object** (`undefined`, `null`, a number, a string, a boolean, a function without the members), **or** its `on` is not callable, **or** its `off` is not callable. In that state: `createGestureSession` still returns an instance; `install` returns `false` with **no source call**; `begin` reports **`'not-installed'`**; `gesture()` returns `null`; `stats()` reports zeros; `dispose()` reports `{removed: 0, complete: true}`; **and nothing ever throws.** **A source whose `on` THROWS** behaves the same at that call (`install` returns `false`, **no ledger entry**, no listener) — **the throw is swallowed at the seam, not propagated into the consumer's event handler.** **A source whose `off` throws at `dispose()`** yields the per-call catch of `§2.3` item 7 (4).

**Item 7 — no value is ever validated, coerced, defaulted, rounded or clamped.** `gesture.set(v)` stores **whatever it is given, verbatim**, including `undefined`/`null`/`NaN`/a `Symbol`/a function/an object; the session performs **no comparison, no arithmetic and no `typeof` check on a value**. **This is what keeps every bound, default and axis out of this unit** (`§1` item 2, P-5): **a session that validated a value would be a session holding policy.**

**Item 8 — nothing survives a gesture.** At a terminal the active record is discarded: `active` becomes `false`, `outcome` keeps the terminal's word, `value` keeps the committed value **as a readable record of the gesture that just ended** but is **never read by a later gesture** (a new `begin` sets `value` back to `undefined`), and **no element-keyed value, cache, memo or map exists anywhere.** Only `gestureSeq` (a number) and `commitCount` (a number) persist for the instance's lifetime.

### 2.5 What `U-GUTTER` (`E3`) and `U-RELOCATE` (`E4`) may call — the ONE signature list

**This is the section `§2.1`'s delegate clause serves, and it is stated once so both specs can be written against it:**

1. **`createGestureSession({source, commit}) → GestureSession`** — once per session.
2. **`session.install(element, {capture?, onStart?, onMove?, onEnd?, onCancel?}) → boolean`** — once per control element; `true` only on first install.

   **THE INJECTED SOURCE'S CAPTURE MEMBER, NAMED HERE (`⟶ RECORDED 2026-09-27`, the `D-1`/`ADV-GS-3` amendment — this item is the ONE signature list `E3`/`E4` write their specs against, so the member's NAME is derived from `§2.5` and not from the module's bytes).** **The injected source MAY supply `capturePointer?(element: GestureElement): void` — the OPTIONAL capture capability declared on `EventSource` at `§2.1`.** **The session's SINGLE capture call per opted-in gesture IS that member** (invoked at most once, inside `begin`, after establishment, only for a control installed with a truthy `capture` — `§2.3` item 6(b)). **A source WITHOUT the member simply takes THE DECLARED DEGRADATION: zero capture calls, no throw and no diagnostic needed, with the gesture establishing and terminating normally** (`§2.1`'s `EventSource` block; the probing row is **`M-8b`**). **`{capture?}` is therefore a complete opt-in instruction: the flag is the consumer's, the capability is the SOURCE's, and the count is the session's.**
3. **`session.begin(element) → BeginResult`** — normally left to the session's own listener; exposed so a test or a consumer-driven start can establish deterministically.
4. **`session.end(element, gesture, value?) → TerminalResult`** — **one commit** (`committed: true`).
5. **`session.reset(element, gesture, value) → TerminalResult`** — **one commit of the SUPPLIED value** (`committed: true`, `outcome: 'reset'`).
6. **`session.cancel(element, gesture?) → TerminalResult`** — **zero commits** (`committed: false`).
7. **`session.dispose() → {removed, complete}`** — the baseline restore; idempotent; permanently inert afterwards.
8. **`session.gesture() → GestureStats | null`** and **`session.stats() → SessionStats`** and **`session.disposed: boolean`** — read-only state, no policy.
9. **Inside the hooks, and only there:** **`gesture.set(value) → GestureHandle`** (total, validating nothing) and the read-only handle fields **`id` · `element` · `active` · `outcome` · `value`**.
10. **Inside `commit`, the two arguments:** **`(gesture: GestureHandle, value: unknown)`** — where **`gesture.outcome`** is the `'end'` vs `'reset'` discriminator and **`value`** is the gesture's final value.
11. **Module-level, if they want the seam directly:** **`installGestureListeners(source, element, onStart)`** / **`detachGestureListeners(source, element, handler)`** / **`POINTER_TYPES`**.

**Nothing else exists.** There is **no** `commit()` method on the session, **no** `setPointerCapture`/`releasePointerCapture` method, **no** `boundsFor`/`axisFor`/`isResizable`/`defaultSizeFor`/`threshold`/`candidatesFor`/`resolveTarget`/`onReveal` parameter, **no** value getter that computes, **no** promise, **no** subscription, **no** store, and **no** options object that could smuggle a census, a selector, a default or a policy predicate in.

### 2.6 The sibling properties — each DERIVABLE FROM THE SESSION, and none re-expressed

**What this subsection is.** `docs/pending.md` §A (`SCH-6`, `SCH-7`) and `docs/next-steps.md`'s `## OPEN` rows `E3`/`E4` pin **seven** properties that the two composing controllers must satisfy **through** this session. **This filing asserts that EVERY ONE of them is expressible through the single session shape above** — with the clause that expresses it and the row here that pins it — and **it finds NO property that cannot be expressed**.

| # | The sibling property (source) | The clause that expresses it | The row here |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **1** | **one commit per gesture** (`E3`, `E4`, `SCH-6`, `SCH-7`) | **`§2.3` item 4/5**: the session invokes `commit` exactly once per gesture reaching `end`/`reset`, and counts it | **`I-2`**, **`M-10`**, **`P-GS-SM-3`** |
| **2** | **cancel ⇒ ZERO commits and zero sink writes** (`E3`) | **`§2.3` item 4** (`cancel` ⇒ `committed:false`) — and *"zero sink writes"* is **the consumer's own conclusion** from *"its `commit` callback did not run"*, so the controller's sink is unreachable by construction | **`I-3`**, **`M-11`**, **`P-GS-SM-3`** |
| **3** | **reset ⇒ exactly ONE commit of the SUPPLIED default** (`E3`) | **`§2.3` item 4** (`reset` ⇒ exactly one commit of the caller's value, `outcome:'reset'`) | **`I-3b`**, **`M-12`**, **`P-GS-SM-3`** |
| **4** | **no capture before establishment** (`E3`, `E4`, `H-r9`) | **`§2.3` item 6**: capture is one call inside `begin`, after the tracking listeners open, and only for an opted-in control | **`I-7`**, **`I-8`**, **`M-14`/`M-15`**, **`P-GS-IM-5`** |
| **5** | **interrupt/cancel leaves NO retained sinks and no listeners** (`E4`) | **`§2.3` item 2(c)/item 7**: detach-all-three happens **before** any consumer code, so nothing survives a cancel; and the session **retains no sink of its own** (it holds the consumer's `commit` only as a construction argument) | **`I-4`**, **`I-5`**, **`M-11`**, **`M-13`**, **`P-GS-IM-6`** |
| **6** | **no second gesture authority** (`V-13`'s class; `SCH-6`'s reason code) | **`§2.3` item 3**: one active gesture per instance, `'busy'` on a second start, and **no consumer-callable `commit()`** (`§0A` note 5) | **`I-1`**, **`F-2`**, **`P-GS-IM-2`** |
| **7** | **reveal written EXACTLY ONCE per gesture AT GESTURE END** (`E4`; a deliberate strengthening) | **`§2.6` item 7's composition rule, stated as a REQUIREMENT on `U-RELOCATE`**: its `onReveal` **must be invoked only inside its own `commit` callback** — the session's single per-gesture commit call at a terminal — **and never from `onMove`/`onStart`**; the session supplies the single-call point and **cannot itself enforce a controller's internal write discipline** | the **`U-RELOCATE` spec's own row** — **this spec pins only the mechanism it is derivable from**: **`I-2`**, **`I-3b`**, `§2.6` item 7 |

**THE F-1 PRECONDITION, NAMED AND NOT ASSUMED (item 7's sibling statement).**

- **`F-11` — THE STRUCTURAL HALF, satisfied HERE and asserted as a row.** *No code path in this module can retarget a press's `click`*: the module contains **no `click` listener, no `click` token, no element re-lookup and no listener on any element other than the control** (`R-2`/`R-7`), the start type is **pinned** to `pointerdown` (`§0A` note 10), and **the module's only capture call is inside `begin` and only for an opted-in control** (`§2.3` item 6, `I-7`/`I-8`). **This half IS provable on the node suite** and `R-2`/`R-7`/`R-8` are its rows.
- **`F-12` — THE BEHAVIOURAL HALF, `PRECONDITION-GATED`, and NOT CLAIMED.** *"The `F-1` BEHAVIOURAL row requires the extended harness (`U-DIVERGENCE-EXT`, ledger row `C2`), which is a **NAMED PRECONDITION**, not an assumed capability."* **`C2` has not landed** (its row is `BLOCKED`), so **`F-12` is `NOT-RUNNABLE-HERE`**: **no pass may claim the fork's retargeting criterion is PROVEN from this unit, from the node suite, or from the existing `divergence` leg** (the pinned `N = 9` identity leg cannot assert an attribute or an event-routing fact — the gate record's own precondition row). **This spec offers NO `[D]` row and NO `[U]` row** (`§5.2`), and **a DONE row that reports `F-12` as green is a review finding.**

## 3. Behaviour (every state / fail-state)

**Layer labels:** **[T]** harness-side · **[H]** host-side · **[U]** the `ui` leg (not offered) · **[D]** the divergence harness (not claimed). **Every row in this file is a `[T]` row**, and **every row is a contract row for the TestWriter; none is a measurement this pass took.** **Every row carries an id and a `Pinned by` citation.**

### 3.1 Valid / happy states

| id | State | Trigger (exact) | Required behaviour | Pinned by | Layer |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **M-1** | **A control is installed, and its start listener is on ITS OWN element** | a recording source; `session.install(elA)` and `session.install(elB)` | **exactly TWO `on` calls**, both with type `'pointerdown'`; **one carries `elA` and one `elB`** (identity assertions, `toBe`); **zero `on` calls carry any other element**; both installs return `true` | §2.3 item 1 | `[T]` |
| **M-2** | **A gesture is established by the control's OWN listener** | fire the recorded `pointerdown` handler installed for `elA` | a gesture is active on `elA`; `session.stats().gestures === 1`; the handle's `id === 1`; **the handler was called with no event argument the module reads** (the row passes an opaque event object and asserts **no property read** — a `Proxy` event with throwing traps changes nothing) | §2.3 item 1(d), §2.4 item 1 | `[T]` |
| **M-3** | **The session's callbacks receive the consumer's values VERBATIM** | `begin`, `onMove` sets `{k: 1}` then a `Symbol` then `undefined`, then `end` | `commit` receives **(the terminal handle, the last value)** **by identity** (`toBe` for the object; `Object.is` for the `NaN`/`-0` cases); the values are **never coerced, copied, frozen or validated** | §2.4 item 7, §2.3 item 4 | `[T]` |
| **M-4** | **One commit for a normal end** | a full `begin` → `pointermove` → `pointerup` sequence | **⟶ CORRECTED 2026-09-27 (the `ADV-GS-35` amendment — ANNOTATE-NEVER-REWRITE: the AS-FILED requirement is kept visible below and is SUPERSEDED, because it asked for a reading the surface cannot produce).** **THE AS-FILED FORM WAS:** *"`commit` invoked **exactly once**; `session.stats().commits === 1`; `gesture().commits === 1`; `handle.outcome === 'end'`; `session.stats().active === false`"*. **THE RULED REQUIREMENT IS THE SAME CLAIM READ FROM THE SURFACE THAT CAN PRODUCE IT: `commit` invoked exactly ONCE (the injected sink's own call record), `session.stats().commits === 1`, `handle.outcome === 'end'`, `session.stats().active === false` — and NOT `gesture().commits`, because the terminal discarded the record so `gesture()` is `null` after it (`§2.4` item 8, `M-17`; the same ruling as `ADV-GS-22`(b) at `§5.5.1 P-GS-SM-3`).** | §2.3 items 4/5 | `[T]` |
| **M-5** | **A second gesture is a NEW gesture** | two full sequences on the same element | ids `1` then `2`; commits `2`; **the second handle's `value` starts as `undefined` regardless of the first gesture's value** — nothing carried | §2.4 item 8, §0A note 8 | `[T]` |
| **M-6** | **The tracking listeners exist only inside the window** | record every source call across `begin` → `move` → `up` | `on(el, 'pointermove')`, `on(el, 'pointerup')`, `on(el, 'pointercancel')` **after** `isConnected` and **before** `onStart`; then on the terminal **`off(el, 'pointermove')`, `off(el, 'pointerup')`, `off(el, 'pointercancel')` in that order**; **the sequence is asserted as an ordered call log** | §2.3 item 2, §0A note 11 | `[T]` |
| **M-7** | **`capture: true` opts ONE control in, and the call happens after establishment** | two controls, `{capture: true}` on `elA` and `{}` on `elB`; establish on `elA`, then on `elB` | exactly **ONE** capture call, on `elA`, and it appears in the call log **after the three tracking `on` calls**; **ZERO capture calls for `elB`** | §2.3 item 6 | `[T]` |
| **M-8** | **`capture` truthiness is the opt-in** | `install` with `capture: 'yes'` (truthy) and with `capture: 0` (falsy) | the `'yes'` control gets exactly one capture call after establishment; the `0` control gets **zero** | §2.3 item 6(a)/(b) | `[T]` |
| **M-7b** | **`capturePointer` is the source's ONE capture member, DECLARED on `EventSource`** | `⟶ RECORDED 2026-09-27 (the `D-1`/`ADV-GS-3` amendment; id kept within the `M-*` family and no existing id renumbered): a typed recording source carrying `capturePointer`, `install(el, {capture: true})`, establish | **exactly ONE call reaching that member**, carrying **the gesture's OWN element**, appearing **after the three tracking `on` calls** and **after `isConnected`** — the member is the capability `§2.1`'s `EventSource` now declares and `§2.5` item 2 now names | §2.1 (`EventSource`), §2.5 item 2, §2.3 item 6(b) | `[T]` |
| **M-8b** | **A capture-LESS source is the DECLARED DEGRADATION: an opt-in with no member still establishes and still terminates** | `⟶ RECORDED 2026-09-27 (the `D-1`/`ADV-GS-3` amendment; the row the `D-1` remedy (iii) owes): `install(el, {capture: true})` on a source whose surface has **NO** `capturePointer` member, then `begin`, then `pointerup` | **ZERO capture calls and NO throw, no diagnostic and no refusal**: the gesture **establishes** (`begin` ⇒ `{ok:true}`), opens the three tracking listeners, **terminates normally** at its terminal (the declared commit count for that path), restores the baseline to `1`, and **no consumer boundary sees an error** — the absence is the degradation, never a failure | §2.1 (`EventSource`), §2.5 item 2, §2.3 item 6(a), §2.4 item 6 | `[T]` |

| **M-11** | **`cancel` detaches BEFORE the consumer's hook runs** | `begin`; in `onCancel`, read the source call log | at the moment `onCancel` runs, the log **already contains the three `off` calls** — the baseline is restored before consumer code (§0A note 11) | §2.3 item 2(c), item 7 | `[T]` |
| **M-12** | **`reset` commits the SUPPLIED value exactly once, with its own outcome** | `begin`, `set(userValue)`, then `reset(el, handle, suppliedDefault)` | `commit` called **exactly once** with `suppliedDefault` (NOT `userValue`); `handle.outcome === 'reset'`; `committed === true` | §2.3 item 4, §0A note 6 | `[T]` |

| **M-14** | **`begin` is established only when the element is connected** | an `isConnected` seam returning `true`, then one returning `undefined` | both `begin` calls succeed and attach the three tracking listeners; `isConnected` was called **exactly once per attempt** | §2.4 item 5 | `[T]` |
| **M-15** | **The counters are the session's own and are readable** | after the M-4 sequence, then M-9, then M-12 | `stats()` reports `{installed, sourceCalls, gestures: 3, commits: 2, active: false, gestureId: 0, lastCode: 'ok'}`; **`sourceCalls` equals the number of `on` + `off` + `isConnected` entries in the recorded log** | §0A note 12, §2.1 `stats` | `[T]` |
| **M-16** | **Isolation: an element only ANOTHER session installed cannot be established here** | two sessions, one source each; `sessionA.install(elX)`; `sessionB.begin(elX)` | `sessionB.begin` returns **`{ok:false, code:'not-installed'}`** and `sessionB` makes **zero** source calls; **`sessionA` is unaffected** (`stats().active === false` before its own start) — the row's message names `MULTI-GRAPH-ISOLATION` | §0 ruling 11, §1 item 6 | `[T]` |
| **M-17** | **`gesture()` is the active reading and `null` when idle** | read `gesture()` before any gesture, during one, and after a terminal | `null` · a `GestureStats` with `active:true`, the live `id`, `outcome: null`, `commits: 0` · after the terminal, **`null` again** with the terminal's facts readable through the handle and `stats()` | §2.1, §2.4 item 8 | `[T]` |
| **M-18** | **`⟶ RECORDED 2026-09-27 (the `D-9`/`ADV-GS-12` amendment — the `P-4` RULE SET this table previously lacked): a session that has installed a control, established a gesture, moved, and terminated it authors NO UI content** | `⟶ RECORDED 2026-09-27: drive a full lifecycle (install → `begin` → `pointermove` → `end`) over a recording source whose elements are plain objects with a **write-recording** surface (a `setAttribute`/`classList`/`textContent` spy triple), and then assert **every prohibited WRITE TOKEN is absent from the unit's own change set** | **at runtime: the session makes NO write call of any kind on any element it is handed** — no attribute write, no class write, no text write, no markup write, no insert/append, no `style` write — **and the element's own recorded write log stays EMPTY**; **statically: `R-12` finds no prohibited write token in the module's bytes or in this unit's own test file's controlled corpora** (`F-11`'s scan shape) | §2.2 **P-4**, `R-12`, `R-8`, `I-6`, `§7` item 8 | `[T]` + static |

**⟶ RECORDED 2026-09-27 — THE `P-4` RULE SET, in the file's own prohibition-table shape (the `D-9`/`ADV-GS-12` remedy; a RULE LIST, not a `§5.5.1` register row).** The row above (**`M-18`**) is the runtime half; the static half is **`R-12`** (`§3.4`), which enumerates the prohibited write tokens — `setAttribute`, `removeAttribute`, `classList`, `className`, `textContent`, `innerText`, `innerHTML`, `outerHTML`, `insertAdjacentHTML`, `insertAdjacentText`, `createElement`, `appendChild`, `insertBefore`, `removeChild`, and the `style`-write family — and scans for them over the module's whole bytes (comments included, token-assembly joined) **and** over this unit's OWN controlled corpora, with a positive control (a corpus spelling one of them raw/joined/in a comment **FAILS**) and a negative control (the module's legitimate text — its four event types, its seven codes, its parameter names — **PASSES**). **What this closes: `setAttribute`/`textContent`/`classList`/`insertAdjacentHTML` appeared in NO rule list before this pass, and `R-8`'s own text forbids a class write its rules did not implement — `P-4`, `R-8` and the rows behind them now agree, and `M-18`+`R-12` are the pair.** **No register row is added, no register term moves, and no existing row id is renumbered.**

### 3.2 Documented fail-states / non-happy states

| id | Fail-state | Trigger (exact) | Required behaviour | Pinned by | Layer |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **F-1** | **The rejected source shape — a delegated/selector/hostile attach** | a consumer tries the REJECTED shape: an options object carrying `selectors`, `threshold`, a `data-zone` name, or an `addEventListener`-shaped field | **the fields are IGNORED** (the session reads only `capture` and the four hooks — `onStart` · `onMove` · `onEnd` · `onCancel`; *(`⟶ CORRECTED 2026-09-27`, THE GATE-7 PROOFREADER PASS: this cell AS FILED read *"the five hooks"* and the as-filed word is kept visible; the OPT-IN `capture` is the fifth FIELD but not a hook, and it is named separately in the same sentence. No hook was added or removed.)*) — **there is no code path by which a selector or a threshold reaches behaviour**; and the session's behaviour is **byte-identical** to the same `install` without them | P-1, P-7, `R-1`, §4.4 S-1 | `[T]` |
| **F-2** | **A second start while a gesture is active** | `begin(elA)` then `begin(elA)` (and a `pointerdown` on `elB` while `elA`'s gesture is active) | both second calls return **`{ok:false, code:'busy'}`**; **zero extra source calls**; the ACTIVE gesture is untouched (same handle id, same value, no extra commit) | §2.3 item 3, §2.4 item 3 | `[T]` |
| **F-3** | **A consumer hook or `commit` THROWS** | (a) `onMove` throws; (b) `onEnd` throws; (c) `commit` throws | the throw **propagates to the caller** (the session does not swallow consumer errors, unlike its own seam errors); **and in every case the three tracking listeners are ALREADY detached**, the gesture is no longer active, the commit counts as the gesture's ONE commit ((c) only), and **no listener is left behind** | §0A note 11, §2.3 items 4/5, `I-3c` | `[T]` |
| **F-4** | **A terminal on a STALE handle** | `begin` (id 1) → `end` → `begin` (id 2) → `end(el, <id-1 handle>)` | the stale call returns **`{ok:false, code:'stale', committed:false}`**, **commits NOTHING**, and **the id-2 gesture is STILL ACTIVE** (a refusal never half-terminates); the same for a handle whose `element` differs from the passed `element` | §2.4 item 4 | `[T]` |
| **F-5** | **A terminal with NO active gesture** | `end(el, handle)` / `reset(el, handle, v)` / `cancel(el, handle)` when idle | `{ok:false, code:'no-gesture', committed:false}`; **zero commits**, **zero source calls** | §2.3 item 4 | `[T]` |
| **F-6** | **`begin` on an element that was never installed here** | `begin(elZ)` on a fresh session | `{ok:false, code:'not-installed'}`; **zero source calls**; nothing recorded | §2.3 item 1(c), M-16 | `[T]` |
| **F-7** | **A DISCONNECTED element at `begin`** | `isConnected(el)` returns exactly `false` | `{ok:false, code:'disconnected'}`; **NO tracking listener is attached** (the log shows only the `isConnected` call); **no gesture exists**; `onStart` does not run | §2.4 item 5 | `[T]` |
| **F-8** | **An UNUSABLE or throwing source** | `createGestureSession(undefined)` · `{source: undefined}` · `{source: 42}` · `{source: {}}` · a source whose `on` throws · a source whose `off` throws · a source whose `isConnected` throws | **construction never throws**; `install` returns `false` with **no source call**; `begin` reports `'not-installed'`; `stats()` reports zeros; `end`/`reset`/`cancel` report `{ok:false, code:'no-gesture', committed:false}`; a throwing `on` leaves **no ledger entry**; a throwing `isConnected` is swallowed and the gesture PROCEEDS (item 5); a throwing `off` yields `{removed: <successes>, complete: false}` | §2.4 item 6, §2.3 item 7(4), §0A note 9 | `[T]` |
| **F-9** | **The session is unreachable by `provident.dispatch`, and exposes no MCP surface** | a static + call-shape probe: the module's exported surface contains **no** tool descriptor, no `RpcMethod` string, no IPC channel name, no `list_targets` handle | the module's value exports are **exactly the four of `§2.1`**, the module **registers nothing**, and **no `src/**` file imports it**: *"no session handle in `list_targets`; a dispatch on a session-owned element's node yields the ordinary handler result with no session side effect"* | §0 ruling 10, P-6, `R-6` | `[T]` + static |
| **F-9(b)** | **The six registration sites are untouched** | the diff/existence probe | `ALL_TOOLS` (21 names) · `RpcMethod` (21) · `MUTATING_METHODS` (7) · `VALID_GROUPS` (5) · the renderer RPC switch · the preload bridge are **unchanged**, asserted **by SET EQUALITY AGAINST THE NAMES where a name-complete row exists**, never by a bare count | §0 ruling 9, `R-6(c)`, §4.4 S-2 | static |
| **F-9c** | **A `pointerdown` on a CHILD of an installed control** | `begin(elA)` (active); then the recorded `pointerdown` handler for `elA` is fired again (the child's own handler case) | `{ok:false, code:'busy'}` — **the session starts NO second gesture and opens NO second listener set**; the child's OWN handler is **not** the session's business and the session never inspects the event to decide this | §2.3 item 3, §2.4 items 1/3 | `[T]` |
| **F-10** | **`capture` is read for truthiness only, and NEVER before establishment** | a full call log over `install` + `begin` (opted in) + terminal, with the log ordered | the capture call appears **exactly once**, **after** the three tracking `on` calls and **after** `isConnected`; **there is no capture call before `begin` at all**, and none for a falsy `capture` | §2.3 item 6, P-3, `I-7` | `[T]` |

### 3.3 Invariants that hold in every state

| id | Invariant | Why it is here | Pinned by |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **I-1** | **THE SINGLE GESTURE AUTHORITY: one session has AT MOST ONE active gesture, and no consumer-reachable call can commit outside a terminal** — the session exposes no `commit()` method, and `commit` is invoked only by the session's `end`/`reset` paths | Prohibitions P-5/P-10; validity `V-13`'s remedy; the `SCH-6` reason code | §2.3 item 3, §0A note 5, `§5.5.1 P-GS-IM-2` |
| **I-2** | **EXACTLY ONE `commit` PER GESTURE that reaches a terminal-state of `end`/`reset`** — for every gesture, over its whole life, the session invokes `commit` **once and only once**; `stats().commits` increments by exactly one per gesture, and the sum equals the number of `end`+`reset` terminals across the session | THE heart of this unit; the sibling property `U-GUTTER`/`U-RELOCATE` derive from | §2.3 items 4/5, `M-4`/`M-10`/`M-12`, `§5.5.1 P-GS-SM-3` |
| **I-3** | **ZERO `commit` INVOCATIONS ON CANCEL** — `cancel`, a `pointercancel`, and a `dispose()` during a gesture all invoke `commit` **zero times**, for any value state | The sibling property *"cancel ⇒ zero commits"* | §2.3 item 4, `M-9`/`M-11`, `§5.5.1 P-GS-SM-3` |
| **I-3b** | **EXACTLY ONE `commit` ON RESET, OF THE SUPPLIED VALUE, with `outcome:'reset'`** — never zero, never two, never the previously-set value when a `value` argument is supplied | The sibling property *"reset ⇒ exactly ONE commit of the supplied default"* | §2.3 item 4, `M-12`, `§0A` note 6 |
| **I-3c** | **A THROWING `commit` IS STILL THE GESTURE'S ONE COMMIT, AND IS NEVER RETRIED** — the throw propagates; the session does not swallow it (it swallows only its OWN seam errors), does not retry, and does not invoke `commit` again for that gesture | Keeps the count falsifiable in the hostile case | §2.3 item 5, `F-3`(c) |
| **I-4** | **THE TRACKING LISTENERS EXIST ONLY BETWEEN START AND TERMINAL: for an installed, idle control the session holds EXACTLY ONE listener on it (the start listener), and during a gesture EXACTLY FOUR; at every terminal the tracking three are detached BEFORE any consumer code runs** | Prohibitions P-3/P-7; the ledger's *"opened ONLY between start and end"*; `§0A` note 11 | §2.3 item 2, `M-6`/`M-11`, `§5.5.1 P-GS-IM-4` |
| **I-5** | **THE BASELINE RESTORE IS EXACT AND REPORTED: every `on(element, type, handler)` the session made is matched by exactly one `off(element, type, handler)` with the same three values, and after `dispose()` the session's own contribution is ZERO listeners; `DisposeReport` reports the successful detaches and sets `complete:false` if any detach failed** | The ledger's *"`dispose()` restoring the listener baseline"*; `§0A` note 3 | §2.3 item 7, `M-13`, `§5.5.1 P-GS-IM-6` |
| **I-6** | **NO DOCUMENT-DELEGATED LISTENER AND NO DOM ACCESS, EVER: the module contains no `document`/`window`/`globalThis`-rooted access, no `addEventListener`/`removeEventListener`, no `closest`/`querySelector`/`querySelectorAll`/`getElementById` — in any form (raw, assembled from literals, commented, or realm-rooted)** | The `SCH-2` acceptance line (*zero document-delegated listeners · zero `closest`/`querySelector` calls*) | `R-1`/`R-2`/`R-7`, §4.4 S-1 |
| **I-7** | **ZERO CAPTURE CALLS BEFORE ESTABLISHMENT: the module's only capture call is inside `begin`, after the tracking listeners are attached; there is no capture call from an `install`, from the start listener's own turn, or for a control that did not opt in, and the call count per gesture is ZERO or ONE — never more** | The `SCH-2` acceptance line + `S-d9`'s narrow reading (binding, `Q2` = (a)) | §2.3 item 6, `M-7`/`M-8`, `F-10`, `§5.5.1 P-GS-IM-5` |
| **I-8** | **EVERY listener and the capture call go through the INJECTED SOURCE — the module never calls `addEventListener`, `removeEventListener`, `setPointerCapture` or `releasePointerCapture` on anything, and it never obtains an element except as an argument** | The `A-d3` seam is the point of the unit | `R-7`, `R-3`, §2.2 P-3, `§5.5.1 P-GS-TP-1` |
| **I-9** | **ELEMENT IDENTITY IS BY REFERENCE: the ledger is keyed by the element object, the handler is bound to that object, and a structurally-identical but distinct object is a different control; the session never derives an element from a string** | `§0A` note 2 | §2.4 item 2, `M-1`/`M-16`, `R-5` |
| **I-10** | **TOTALITY AT THE SEAM: `createGestureSession` NEVER throws for ANY argument; `install`/`begin`/`end`/`reset`/`cancel`/`dispose`/`gesture`/`stats` return their declared shape for every input — a hostile, absent or throwing SOURCE is degraded, never propagated** | Admission clause (B)'s totality standard applied to an injected seam | §2.4 item 6, `F-8`, `§5.5.1 P-GS-TP-1` |
| **I-11** | **NEVER A GEOMETRY, COORDINATE OR MAGNITUDE CLAIM: no row of this unit may assert a rendered-geometry, layout, paint, coordinate or click-retargeting-in-a-browser property, and **no green of this unit may be reported as one**; the module reads NO pointer coordinate and NO event field at all** | The mandatory geometry clause (`§0` ruling 7); `S-d9`'s *"origin REACHABILITY, not magnitude-equivalence"*; `RK-19`'s false-green class | §2.3 item 5/7, §2.4 item 1, `R-8`, `§5.2`, `§7` item 5, `§5.5.1 P-GS-TP-2` |
| **I-12** | **NO STORE, NO PERSISTENCE, NO CROSS-GESTURE CARRY: no file, no `localStorage`, no store object, no cache, no memo, no module-level mutable state; the instance keeps at most ONE gesture record and DISCARDS it at every terminal; only two monotonic counters (`gestureSeq`, `commitCount`) and the installed-element ledger outlive a gesture** | The `SCH-2` acceptance line (*no store, no persistence*) made testable | §0A note 8, §2.4 item 8, `M-5`/`M-13`, `R-2`, `§5.5.1 P-GS-IM-6` |
| **I-12b** | **RE-ENTRANCY CANNOT CORRUPT THE BASELINE OR THE COUNT: while a terminal is running, the gesture is ALREADY inactive and its tracking listeners are ALREADY detached — so a consumer hook or `commit` that re-enters `begin`/`end`/`cancel`/`dispose` sees a consistent state, cannot double-commit, and cannot leave a listener behind; a `dispose()` from inside a hook is honoured and the terminal completes without a further commit** | The hostile-consumer class the adversarial seed `A-6` will drive | §0A note 11, §2.3 item 4, `F-3` |
| **I-13** | **NO AMBIENT READ AND NO IMPORT: the module reads no ambient global (`document`, `window`, `globalThis`, `self`, `matchMedia`, `getComputedStyle`, `activeElement`, `Date`, `Math.random`, `process.env`) and imports NOTHING — not the engine, not the shim, not a sibling mechanism** | Admission clause (B)/(C); the portability reason the seam exists | `R-2`, `R-3`, `R-4`, `§0` ruling 12 |

### 3.4 The STATIC rows — the rows `§2.2`'s prohibition table cites, ENUMERATED

**What this subsection is, and why it exists.** `§2.2` cites static rows for **every one of its ten prohibitions**. **A prohibition citing *"a static source row"* with no id is not a row** (the sibling reviews' recurring finding), so **every static claim in this file has an id here**, and **each scan is closed against the evasion class** (token assembly, comment-carrying, realm-rooted computed access) by `§4.4 S-1`'s stop condition. **Every row here is `static`-layer: it reads the unit's own FILES, never the module's runtime behaviour.**

| id | Row (a TestWriter authors this) | Pinned by | Layer |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **R-1** | **The anti-evasion VOCABULARY row (P-1, P-4, P-5, P-7).** *Over the MODULE's source (`src/shared/gesture-session.ts`) INCLUDING its comments, no occurrence, in any form, of: a selector string (`selectors`, a CSS-ish selector literal), a `threshold` token, a zone/pane/tab/region/dashboard vocabulary token, a `data-zone`/`pane-collapse-toggle`-style attribute name, a `localStorage`/`sessionStorage`/store/cache/memo token, or a `closest`/`querySelector`/`querySelectorAll`/`getElementById` token.* **The scan reads a NORMALIZED view in which string-literal concatenation is JOINED before scanning (`'thres' + 'hold'`, a template literal with substituted parts, a token split across a line break) and COMMENTS ARE SCANNED LIKE CODE**, with a word/identifier BOUNDARY rule. **SCOPE, stated because this row's own spelling collides with the module's legitimate event names:** the module **must** contain `'pointerdown'`/`'pointermove'`/`'pointerup'`/`'pointercancel'` and its own result codes, and the TEST FILE must carry the banned spellings inside this row's own control data and its assertion messages — so the scan's scope is **the module file (whole, comments included)** plus **this row's OWN controlled corpora**, and a **whole-file negative over the test file is DELIBERATELY DROPPED** (a whole-file scan of a file that must contain the spellings can only fail). **Controls, both required:** a **POSITIVE control** (a corpus spelling a banned token raw, joined, and inside a comment **FAILS**) and a **NEGATIVE control** (this unit's own legitimate text — the four event types, the seven codes, the parameter names — **PASSES**). **A row that passes for a module spelling any banned token in any of those three forms is UNFALSIFIED and must not be filed.** | P-1/P-4/P-5/P-7, `I-6`, `I-12`, §4.4 S-1 | static |
| **R-2** | **The forbidden-ACCESS row (P-2, P-3, P-8, P-9; `I-6`, `I-13`).** *No access in the module is ROOTED IN A BANNED REALM TOKEN OR AN ALIAS OF ONE* — `globalThis['doc' + 'ument']`, `globalThis[name]`, `realm[propName]`, `const g = globalThis; g.document`, a helper returning the realm, **the no-token realm route** (`({}).constructor.constructor('return this')()`, `Reflect.construct`, `Function.prototype.call`-shaped code construction) — **and no ambient read for a value**: `document`, `window`, `globalThis`, `self`, `top`, `parent`, `frames`, `matchMedia`, `getComputedStyle`, `activeElement`, `Date`, `Math.random`, `process`, `node:fs`, `localStorage`, `sessionStorage`, `eval`, `new Function`. **STATED LIMIT, so the row is not written unassertably: a BLANKET ban on `[expr]` is NOT claimed** — a **locally constructed object's** computed access and ordinary **array indexing** carry no banned token and are **deliberately not banned**; **a row that asserts "no bracket notation at all" FAILS this row's own text** and is `S-3`. | P-2/P-3/P-8/P-9, `I-6`, `I-13` | static |
| **R-3** | **The EVENT-WIRING row (P-3, P-7; `I-8`, `I-6`).** *The module performs NO listener attachment and NO capture of its own: it contains no `addEventListener`, no `removeEventListener`, no `setPointerCapture`, no `releasePointerCapture`, and no `on<event>=`-style assignment; and its ONLY calls that could attach, detach or capture are `source.on(...)`, `source.off(...)` and **the SOURCE-SUPPLIED capture member invoked as `capturePointer(element)`** *(⟶ SUPERSEDED 2026-09-27, the `D-4`/`ADV-GS-4` amendment: **the as-filed clause read *"`source.setPointerCapture?.(...)`-shaped calls made inside `begin`"*** — kept visible here, and superseded because the 2026-09-27 capture ruling makes the capability **the SOURCE's own member**, named neutrally, and the module's bytes may carry **no** DOM-specific capture-method token; see the note below this row)*. **Its falsifiable half:** a module containing `addEventListener`/`setPointerCapture` as a call token **FAILS**, and **a capture call reachable from `install` or from a start listener FAILS** (the row drives the ordered call log, `F-10`). **Its honest limit, stated: a text scan cannot prove the ABSENCE of a capture for all control flow** — so the row pairs the token scan with the **runtime ordered-log row** (`F-10`, `M-6`), and **the pair is the row**. | P-3/P-7, `I-8`, `M-6`/`F-10` | static + `[T]` |
**⟶ RECORDED 2026-09-27 (the `D-4`/`ADV-GS-4` amendment — the capture token's comment-carrying closure is `F-11`'s).** **`R-3` STRIPS COMMENTS from the normalized view it scans, so a capture-method token split across comment chunks is read by NEITHER of its halves — that closure belongs to `F-11`, whose scan is the RAW-plus-ASSEMBLED one (`F-11`'s own text: *"the static scan (`R-2`/`R-7`/`R-8`) over the module and the unit's own test file"*, with the token ban biting in raw, assembled and comment-carrying forms).** **What this records, exactly:** the module's ONLY capture-related bytes are the SOURCE-SUPPLIED member's neutral name and the invocation inside `begin` (`§2.3` item 6(b), `§2.1`'s `EventSource` declaration); **no DOM-specific capture-method token may appear in the module in ANY form**, and **the row that must fail a module spelling it is `F-11`, not `R-3`'s comment-stripped scan.**

| **R-4** | **The IMPORT-BOUNDARY row (P-10; `I-13`).** *`src/shared/gesture-session.ts` imports NOTHING* — not `src/main/**`, not `src/renderer/**`, not `electron`, not `node:*`, not `provident-ssr`, **not the shim, not `./zones.js`/`./census.js`, not `./layout-projection.js`/`./owned-list-host.js`/`./slot-host.js`, and not even a type-only import**. **Any import statement at all FAILS the row.** **A later unit that legitimately imports THIS module is not a violation of it** — the row binds THIS module's own imports, and the *"imported by no `src/**` file yet"* claim is `R-5`'s. | P-10, `I-13`, §1 item 6 | static |
| **R-5** | **The ISOLATION / OWNERSHIP row (P-2, P-10; `I-9`; `M-16`).** *The only elements the module ever hands to the source are the exact references passed to `install` and to `begin`* — the row asserts that **no element reaches the source that was not an argument** (a positive control: an implementation that looked an element up, cached one, or closed over one **FAILS**), **that the ledger is keyed by identity** (two structurally identical objects are two controls; the same object twice is one), and **that the session installs into ONE graph's DOM only** — **it never touches the isolated `SecurePanels` scope** (`MULTI-GRAPH-ISOLATION`), evidenced by the module containing **no `secure-panels` import and no second mount reference** and by `M-16`'s cross-session row. | P-2/P-10, `I-9`, §0 ruling 11 | static + `[T]` |
| **R-6** | **The EXPORT-CENSUS row (`§2.1`) — a SET claim, never a count.** *`src/shared/gesture-session.ts` exports EXACTLY the twelve names `§2.1`'s block declares, in its two halves:* **(a) the RUNTIME value exports — exactly `createGestureSession`, `installGestureListeners`, `detachGestureListeners` and `POINTER_TYPES`** (read from the imported namespace's own keys, with a **positive control** that a namespace carrying a fifth value export **FAILS**); **(b) THE TYPE-ONLY NAMES — `EventSource`, `GestureElement`, `GestureHandle`, `GestureOptions`, `GestureOptionsInput`, `SessionOptions`, `SessionStats`, `GestureStats` — asserted as a PRESENCE claim** — **⟶ RE-STATED 2026-09-27 (the `D-5`/`ADV-GS-5` amendment; the as-filed word above was `exactly`): a type-only name is ERASED AT RUN TIME, so a runtime row cannot read the type half's key set at all, and the `EXACTLY`/set-equality form is UNFALSIFIABLE at the type layer — the honest claim is that these EIGHT names are PRESENT and IMPORTABLE, which `§5.2` leg 4 pins as a COMPILE-TIME fact** (the row's own leg-4 driver imports each of the eight as a type, so the file does not compile if any is renamed, removed or left unexported — the `TS2307` diagnostic is NOT suppressed); **the SET-EXACTNESS of the exported TYPE half is a DOC claim owned by `§2.1`'s census (`4 + 8 = 12`), not a row claim**, and **`R-6(a)`'s `EXACTLY` for the RUNTIME value half is unchanged and remains falsifiable through the namespace's own keys**; **(c) THE FIVE-SEAM NEGATIVE** — no tool, no resource, no group, no `VALID_GROUPS` member, no `RpcMethod` member, no `MUTATING_METHODS` entry, no IPC method, asserted **by SET EQUALITY AGAINST THE NAMES where a name-complete row exists** and never by a bare count (`S-2`). **A row asserting only a COUNT without NAMING the names FAILS this row's own text.** | `§2.1`, P-6, §0 rulings 9/10, `F-9(b)` | runtime + type-level + static |

## 4. The red (RCA-1) — what must be written, RUN and REPORTED before any implementation

### 4.1 The red statement

**The red is a NEW test file** — **`tests/gesture-session.test.ts`** (`§0A` note 1) — authored **first**, **RUN**, and its failing set **REPORTED verbatim** before any implementation. **Expected red shape:** `Cannot find module '../src/shared/gesture-session.js'` (or the repo's equivalent module-resolution failure) for every row that imports the module, **plus the static rows that can already be evaluated** ⟶ **CORRECTED 2026-09-27 (the red-set pass — the as-filed list named `R-2`/`R-3`/`R-5` too, but `§3.4` defines those as scans OF the module and they cannot be evaluated before it exists; `§3.4`’s table governs): the module-INDEPENDENT static rows are `R-7`/`R-9`/`R-11` ONLY; `R-1`–`R-6` and `R-8` become evaluable when the module lands; and `R-10`’s existence probe keeps its RED form at red time with the GREEN form as provenance.** **⟶ RECORDED 2026-09-27 (the `D-9`/`ADV-GS-12` amendment): `R-12` (the new `P-4` UI-content write row, `§3.4`) scans the MODULE file and this unit's OWN controlled corpora, so it becomes evaluable exactly when `R-1`–`R-6` and `R-8` do — the module-INDEPENDENT list above (`R-7`/`R-9`/`R-11`) is unchanged, and `R-12` is NOT added to it.** THE AS-FILED LIST FOLLOWS AS PROVENANCE:** (`R-2`/`R-3`/`R-5`/`R-7`/`R-9`/`R-11` — several of which are **evaluable before the module exists**, and several of which become evaluable only once it does, which is exactly what `R-10` records).

**There is NO host-fix branch for this unit**: the module does not exist, so the red is **purely additive**, and **the unit owes no change to any existing file**.

**What a green at the end of this cycle is, and is not.** It is **`[T]` evidence that the lifecycle, the call counts and the baseline restore behave as `§2`/`§3` say over the enumerated drivers**. It is **NOT** evidence that a real pointer gesture works, that any pane resizes, that a listener was removed from a browser's listener table, that a `click` is not retargeted in a real renderer, or that the app behaves differently — **in particular, at the end of this cycle the module is still imported by NO `src/**` file** (`R-7`).

### 4.2 Red-set authoring order

1. **The `§3.5` existence/precondition rows `R-10`/`R-11` FIRST** (they are the red's own premise and are evaluable before the module exists).
2. **Then the `§3.4` static rows `R-1`..`R-9` and `R-12`** (`R-7`/`R-9` are evaluable immediately; `R-1`/`R-2`/`R-3`/`R-5`/`R-6`/`R-8`/`R-12` read the module file and become evaluable **once it exists** — which is what `R-10` records). **⟶ RECORDED 2026-09-27 (`D-9`/`ADV-GS-12`): `R-12` is authored LAST in the `§3.4` block (it is a later-row addendum, not a renumbering) and it is the `P-4` UI-content write row.**
3. **Then the invariants `I-1`..`I-13`**, then **`M-1`..`M-17` and `M-18`** *(⟶ RECORDED 2026-09-27, `D-1`/`D-9`: `M-7b`/`M-8b` sit beside `M-7`/`M-8` and `M-18` closes the `M-*` block — the two families keep their range forms and no existing id is renumbered)*, then **`F-1`..`F-11`** (`F-12` is **not authored as a passing row**, `§3.2`), **in that order**.
4. **The `§5.5.1` PROPERTY REGISTER rows are part of THIS red set** — authored in the **same file**, after the `F-*` rows, **in register order** (`P-GS-IM-1` · `P-GS-IM-2` · `P-GS-IM-3` · `P-GS-IM-4` · `P-GS-IM-5` · `P-GS-IM-6` · `P-GS-SM-1` · `P-GS-SM-2` · `P-GS-SM-3` · `P-GS-TP-1` · `P-GS-TP-2`). They ride **`npm test` (leg 1)** unchanged and **need no new file, no new script, no `package.json` change and no dependency.**
5. **RUN and REPORT** the failing set verbatim — the module-resolution failure, plus every static/existence row that can already be evaluated, **plus which register rows ran and which stopped un-run**.
6. **Then** implement the least code that makes them green; **then** re-run and record. **No row may be edited to reach green**; **a row found wrong is corrected IN THIS SPEC first, with the old text kept as `SUPERSEDED`** (annotate-never-rewrite).

**The register's own stop rule binds the red run**: rows are evaluated **sequentially in register order** with **STOP AFTER 5 CONSECUTIVE FAILURES**, so **a red run of a module-absent unit is expected to stop early**, and **the un-run rows must be REPORTED AS FAILURES rather than silently omitted** — **a red run that reports all `400` attempts as executed is the finding, not the expectation.** **The register's `YES` markings are execution DESIGN, not results**: **a row that is `YES` in `§5.5.1` but broken when run is a SPEC FINDING, reported rather than tuned to green.**

### 4.3 What the red is NOT

- **Not a controller test.** No `boundsFor`/`axisFor`/`defaultSizeFor`/`isResizable`/`clampToBounds` (that is `U-GUTTER`), no `candidatesFor`/`resolveTarget`/`onReveal`/drop model/`threshold` arithmetic (that is `U-RELOCATE`). Their rows are their specs' (`§1` item 8).
- **Not a DOM test.** No real `Element`, no `document`, no shim member: **the element is any object the row chooses**, and **the source is the row's own recording object**.
- **Not a geometry or magnitude test.** No coordinate read, no layout, no `getComputedStyle`, no `getBoundingClientRect` — `§4.4 S-4`'s class, and `R-8` is the row that forbids it.
- **Not a real-browser event test, and not a retargeting test.** A `[T]` green cannot show that a real `pointerdown` reaches the handler, that capture behaves in a real renderer, or that a press's `click` is not retargeted — **that is `F-12`, and it is `PRECONDITION-GATED`** (`§2.6`).
- **Not assembled-app evidence.** Layer declaration anchor 1.
- **Not a consumer-vocabulary test.** A row naming a real zone/pane/tab, a selector or a threshold is a **prohibition-1 violation** (its vocabulary may appear **only** inside `R-1`'s own control corpora).

### 4.4 The stop conditions (binding)

| # | Stop condition | Required behaviour |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **S-1** | A row is only satisfiable by a **token scan** (a word list, a regex over source) | **The row must be closed against TOKEN ASSEMBLY and COMMENT-CARRYING before it is authored**: it scans a **normalized** view in which string-literal concatenation is joined **and it scans COMMENTS as code**. **A row that passes for a module spelling a banned token in either form is UNFALSIFIED and must not be filed** (the architect's `SLOTHOST-CONTAINER-SOURCE-IS-INJECTED` rule: *a static prohibition satisfiable by splitting a token is not satisfied*). The row form is `R-1`/`R-2`. |
| **S-2** | A row asserts a prohibition by a **bare COUNT** (*"`ALL_TOOLS` stays 21"*, *"four value exports"*) or claims an **existence/absence about the repo** with no probe | **A count is satisfiable by renaming and passes whether or not the change added a seam.** The row must assert **SET EQUALITY AGAINST THE NAMES** or be replaced by the **import/diff row** that can actually fail (`R-4`/`R-6`/`R-7`), and an existence claim must be an `fs.existsSync`-style **probe whose FAIL is meaningful** (`R-9`/`R-10`/`R-11`). |
| **S-3** | A test needs a **real DOM**, a real element, a `document`, or a shim member to express a row | **Violates the layer declaration's anchor 2 and `§1` item 3** — this unit's entire surface is arguments. Re-write the row against the **recording source** (`M-1`'s form). **A row that needs a DOM is a row this unit cannot have.** |
| **S-4** | A row asserts a **rendered-geometry, coordinate, layout, paint or magnitude** property — or a `[T]` green is to be reported as one | **Violates the mandatory clause and `I-11`** — the claim is **deleted**; this spec offers **no `[U]` row and no `[D]` row**, and `§5.2` states why. **The row may not be moved to another leg silently.** |
| **S-5** | A row is only satisfiable if the session **computes, clamps, rounds, validates or defaults** a value, or if it **reads a pointer coordinate**, or if a consumer can call `commit()` itself | **Violates `I-1`, `§2.4` item 7 and P-5** — that is `U-GUTTER`/`U-RELOCATE` policy entering the session. **Stop and report: it is a contract change needing its own gate, not an implementation choice.** |
| **S-6** | A row needs the **extended divergence harness** (`U-DIVERGENCE-EXT`, `C2`) to pass | **`F-12`'s class.** The row is **`NOT-RUNNABLE-HERE`**, is **not authored as a passing row**, and **may not be moved silently** into the node suite or into the existing `N = 9` divergence leg (whose pinned identity is a HARD constraint, `H-r18`). Re-check `R-11` if the harness lands. |
| **S-7** | A row asserts a session property **on the strength of the injected source's own honesty** (e.g. *"no listener remains in the browser"* because the row's fake source says so) | **The claim is about the RECORDED CALLS, never about a browser's listener table** (layer anchor 5, the gate record's listen-removal limit). The row is re-written as a **call-log assertion** (`M-6`/`M-13`). |
| **S-8** | A consumer obligation is **pulled into this unit** because a composing controller finds it convenient (a bounds callback, a threshold, a drop resolver, a reveal sink, a class taxonomy, a census read) | **Violates P-10 and `H-r6`'s dissolved-edge discipline** — the obligation stays with its owner (`U-GUTTER`/`U-RELOCATE`/`U-CONTAINER`/`U-ZONES`/`U-CENSUS`). **Stop and report: it is a fabricated edge** (`§1` item 7). |
| **S-9** | A row proposes adding an **eighth result code**, a **fourth outcome**, a **fifth event type**, a **`selectors`/`threshold` parameter**, or an **options field** that could smuggle a policy default in | **Each is a contract change.** `§2.3` item 4's union and `§0A` notes 6/10's pins are the contract; **stop and report to the supervisor** (a new code or outcome needs its own gate, exactly as `F-1`'s `''` class did for the sibling `U-ZONES`). |

### 4.5 Delegation gate

**This unit is NOT delegable by this filing.** It needs (a) **this spec to exist** (*done: this filing*), (b) **a TestWriter to have RUN and REPORTED the red set** (`AGENTS.md` item 9), and (c) **the supervisor's go-ahead ordering** — the architect has approved `U-GSESSION` landing **before** `U-GUTTER` (`E3`) (`§0` ruling 14), and **this pass advances no unit status**. **Its `## OPEN` row `E6` keeps whatever status the supervisor's reconciliation gives it**, and **`U-GUTTER`/`U-RELOCATE` are unblocked on this unit's delegate surface (`§2.5`) while their own specs, red sets and gates remain their own** (`RCA-2`).

| 4 | **standalone strict `tsc` over the unit's own test file** — the named leg for `R-6(b)` | a standalone strict `tsc --noEmit` invocation over `tests/gesture-session.test.ts` | **[T]** (the type layer of the unit's own rows) | **Why it is a LEG and not a trio member:** `R-6(b)` asserts eight **TYPE-ONLY** names, and an imported type name is **erased at runtime**, so the runtime half of that row cannot fail, and **leg 2 does not compile `tests/**` at all**. The honest leg is a standalone strict `tsc` over the test file: if any of the eight type names is renamed, removed or left unexported, the file **fails to compile**. **It adds no script to `package.json`, no dependency and no diff-scope row**, and **no register row depends on it.** **⟶ RECORDED 2026-09-27 (the `D-1`(iv) amendment): THIS LEG IS ALSO THE PIN FOR THE CAPTURE MEMBER'S TYPE-LEVEL COVERAGE.** A **typed `EventSource` fixture carrying `capturePointer`** — built on the module's own imported `EventSource` type, driven for a `capture: true` install and asserted to receive the session's ONE capture call at runtime (**`M-7b`**) — is compiled **by this same standalone strict `tsc` leg**. **THE CYCLE, stated so a reader does not mistake the pin for decoration:** the fixture object is **RED for the module under `strict` (excess-property / assignability: the member is declared on `§2.1`'s `EventSource` and used at run time by the module's own capability read, but the module's `EventSource` declaration does not carry it yet — the OWED declaration is named in this pass's report and is a MODULE-side obligation, NOT a `§7a.1` ambiguity) and GREEN once the declaration lands** — **⟶ CORRECTED 2026-09-27 (`ADV-GS-17`, a later pass): THIS RED READING IS SUPERSEDED AND IS KEPT VISIBLE HERE; THE DECLARATION HAS LANDED — `src/shared/gesture-session.ts` declares `capturePointer?(element: GestureElement): void` on its own `EventSource` — so the typed fixture is GREEN and **LEG 4 IS `0` DIAGNOSTICS** (the measured `exit`/diagnostic count for this leg), i.e. the red→green cycle this sentence describes is COMPLETE: the red was `TS2353` (an excess-property/assignability failure) while the declaration was absent, and no MODULE-side `D-1` obligation remains. **The `§2.1` `EventSource` doc block's own `ADV-GS-17` note records the same correction at the declaration site** — so the member has a real red/green cycle even though the root `tsconfig.json` **excludes `tests/**` and leg 2 therefore never reads this file** (`§5.2` leg 2's NAMED LIMIT). |

**THE `[U]` ROW IS **NOT OFFERED** BY THIS UNIT, and the `[D]` ROW IS NOT CLAIMED — and both refusals are structural, stated in the ledger's own words:** *"the contract and its **call counts** are provable here; **any rendered-geometry claim is UNPROVABLE in this repo today** → `ui` only."* **A `[U]` row would be a rendered-geometry, coordinate, layout, capture-in-a-real-renderer or click-retargeting claim** — and **none is falsifiable from this unit's change set**, because (a) the module is **imported by no `src/**` file** (`R-7`), so there is **no rendered surface to observe**, and (b) **the session never reads a coordinate** (`§2.4` item 1), so there is nothing for a measuring leg to measure. **The `ui` leg exists and is green** (`U-REALDOM-BOOT` is `DONE`) **and the divergence leg exists and is green (`R13 RESULT: 9 checks, 0 failures`)** — so **this refusal is not a leg-availability excuse**: it is the structural reason. **The single `[D]`-shaped row this unit's ledger names (`F-1` behavioural, `F-12`) is `PRECONDITION-GATED` on `U-DIVERGENCE-EXT` (`C2`) and is NOT claimed** (`§2.6`, `R-11`). **If a later unit gives this module a shipped consumer, a `[U]` row becomes *possible* — and it would be that unit's row, that unit's spec and that unit's preconditions** (a `ui` green proves *one probe in one real renderer boot* and **nothing else**). **No `§3` row is weakened by the absence of a `[U]` row, because no `§3` row makes a claim of that kind** (`I-11`, `R-8`).

## 5.5 Typed Property register (EXECUTED deterministically — no PBT harness)

**`H-r4` obliges an explicit zero-row/typed-PBT decision per unit. Stated exactly as the sibling specs state it: THIS REPO HAS NO PBT HARNESS.** `package.json`'s `devDependencies` key set is the **five keys** `@types/node`, `electron`, `esbuild`, `typescript`, `vitest` — **no `fast-check`, no `hypothesis`, no property runner**. **This unit is CODE-BEARING** (a real lifecycle mechanism with four value exports, a red set, a test file and a genuine state machine), so **the recorded ZERO-ROW EXEMPTION IS NOT AVAILABLE to it** (`docs/decisions.md`'s ACTIVE row **`PBT-REGISTER-REQUIRED-FOR-CODE-UNITS`** restricts it to genuinely invariant-free / doc-only / config-only / non-JS units). **`§5.5.1` below is therefore a real typed register**, executed by plain deterministic vitest tables and one pinned-seed hand-rolled generator, **with NO new dependency.**

**⟶ THE REGISTER-ENTRY-COUNT RULING, APPLIED HERE (the architect's ruling recorded as `docs/decisions.md`'s ACTIVE row `REGISTER-ENTRY-COUNT-IS-NOT-CAPPED-AND-ARCHIVE-IS-THE-TRUTH-MECHANISM`).** **A register ENUMERATES every discernible testable property of its unit; the per-section threshold (`≤ 8`) is a BREAKDOWN SIGNAL, NOT A CEILING.** **THEREFORE this filing enumerates `11` rows and reports the count as its extent — no property was dropped, merged or left unenumerated to fit a threshold**, and **the unit's own recommendation on the breakdown signal is recorded in `§5.5.2` item 1** (the separable component, named). **No row of this register is an `F-` row**, and **no `§6`/`FS-n` citation appears as a register row.**

### 5.5.1 THE REGISTER — **`11` typed rows, ALL executed by design**

**What this section is, in one sentence.** A **typed register of `11` rows** whose **five genuine quantifications in the ledger's own words** — (i) *one commit per gesture, over EVERY gesture*; (ii) *cancel ⇒ zero commits*; (iii) *reset ⇒ exactly one commit of the supplied value*; (iv) *no capture before establishment, over EVERY configuration*; (v) *the baseline restored by `dispose()`, for EVERY session and every listener set* — are **executed here as quantifications over finite, pinned enumerations**, **hand-rolled and deterministic, with no new dependency, no `fast-check`, no `hypothesis` and no property runner** (the `devDependencies` key set is unchanged — the five keys). Type algebra is `docs/specs/engine-pin.md` §5.5's: **`P-IM`** = invariant · **`P-SM`** = state-machine · **`P-TP`** = totality.

**The ids are THIS UNIT'S OWN KIND and collide with nothing.** The prefix is **`P-GS-*`** (`GS` = this unit, **g**esture **s**ession) — so **a register row is never mistaken for a `§3` row** (whose families are `M-*`/`F-*`/`I-*`/`R-*`) **and never for a sibling's register** (`P-ZN-*`, `P-CN-*`, `P-PJ-*`, `P-LH-*`, `P-SH-*`). The strategy-id prefix is **`S-GS-*`, one per row**, and the one **pinned-seed generator** carries its own id, **`S-GS-SEED-1`**.

**How every row is executed (the strategy discipline, stated once so no row is ambiguous).**

1. **Plain deterministic vitest in this unit's own test file** (`tests/gesture-session.test.ts`, `§4.1`/`§5.1`) — the file the red set already owes, and the file the register **rides as part of the red** (`§4.2` item 4). **No row of this register is executed by a generator library.**
2. **Exhaustive/finite enumeration, or a PINNED-SEED deterministic generator written in plain TypeScript inside the test file.** The only generator in this register is **`S-GS-SEED-1`**'s (used by `P-GS-TP-2`), pinned to **literals in the test file itself** — a hand-rolled 32-bit LCG: **`state₀ = 20260927`**; **`stateₙ₊₁ = (stateₙ · 1664525 + 1013904223) mod 2³²`**; **each draw applies ONE LCG step and the resulting state selects the pool member — `index = stateₙ₊₁ mod pool.length`, with `pool.length = 30`** — so **one pool draw consumes exactly ONE LCG step.** **Stated so no TestWriter reads a two-step or a scaling form into it: there is NO `next(k)` helper in this register** (a pool draw is an index, never a bounded integer). **No `Math.random`, no wall-clock seed, no shrinking, no adaptive input search, no library PRNG.**
3. **Caps, uniform for the whole register:** **≤100 attempts per row, ≤400 attempts in total**, rows evaluated **sequentially in register order**, **STOP AFTER 5 CONSECUTIVE FAILURES** (the running row's remaining attempts are abandoned and no further row starts). **A register row is never refused on the ground that "no PBT harness exists."**
4. **Sample rows are the `§3` rows this register compensates, never replaced by it.** **No `§3` row is weakened, widened or re-scoped by the register.**
5. **No row may be reported as executed if it was sampled** — every row's cell states its input set exactly.
6. **The register's own boundaries, named rather than silently relied on:** (a) **no `Symbol`-keyed or coordinate-carrying event** is needed by any row, because **the session reads no event field at all** (`§2.4` item 1); (b) **no `Proxy` whose traps return inconsistent answers across reads** is in any pool (the hostile shapes are **fixed** table members, deliberately, so no draw is ambiguous); (c) **each row's pool/table is a SUBSET of the input space this contract pins**, and **its silence about a shape it does not list is a stated boundary, not an unrecorded omission**; (d) **`P-GS-SM-3` is the `P-SM` row that carries the ledger's three commit-count properties** (`end`/`cancel`/`reset`), and **no other row may be quoted for them**.

### 5.5.2 The register's honesty block — what is NOT proven here, and the checks this filing RAN

**Item 1 — the row count is an OUTCOME, and the breakdown signal is recorded ONCE.** **`11` rows** were enumerated because **`11` discernible testable property classes exist**; the `≤8` threshold is a **breakdown signal**, not a ceiling (`§5.5`'s ruling block). **THE BREAKDOWN RECOMMENDATION (the signal, stated as the ruling requires):** **`P-GS-TP-1` is the separable component** — its `24` drives bundle **three mechanically distinct totality tests** (the factory's totality, the two module-level seam functions' totality, and the session methods' totality under a hostile source — *the as-filed cell said `94` because it counted ASSERTIONS rather than drives; `§5.5.3` records that reconciliation and the declared count is `24`*) — and it is **the only row whose phases do not share one input domain**; **a future breakdown pass COULD split it into `(i)` factory-totality · `(ii)` seam-function-totality · `(iii)` method-totality rows, each over its own finite domain.** **That is a breakdown of ONE row into three, not a missing property**, and **it is NOT owed by this filing** — no row is added, removed or re-scoped here.

**Item 2 — the FIVE `YES (bounded)` markings *(⟶ CORRECTED 2026-09-27, the `ADV-GS-25` amendment: this item AS FILED read *"the four `YES (bounded)` markings"*, and the as-filed word `four` is kept visible at this site;
